{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_0143BQ2AEw7Vy2PLUaAqqX4Q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CktoBB3GWVV9Z9T6HXWrxM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/bustub/src/primer/hyperloglog.cpp","content":"//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//\n//\n//                         BusTub\n//\n// hyperloglog.cpp\n//\n// Identification: src/primer/hyperloglog.cpp\n//\n// Copyright (c) 2015-2025, Carnegie Mellon University Database Group\n//\n//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//\n\n#include \"primer/hyperloglog.h\"\n#include <cmath>\n\nnamespace bustub {\n\n/** @brief Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLog<KeyType>::HyperLogLog(int16_t n_bits) : cardinality_(0), n_bits_(n_bits) {\n  if (n_bits_ <= 0) {\n    n_bits_ = 0;\n    registers_.resize(1, 0);\n  } else {\n    registers_.resize(1ULL << n_bits_, 0);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes binary.\n *\n * @param[in] hash\n * @returns binary of a given hash\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::ComputeBinary(const hash_t &hash) const -> std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> {\n  return std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY>(hash);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes leading zeros.\n *\n * @param[in] bset - binary values of a given bitset\n * @returns position of leftmost 1 in given binary set (1-indexed from the right, after the bucket bits)\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::PositionOfLeftmostOne(const std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> &bset) const -> uint64_t {\n  // We look for the position of the leftmost 1 in the portion after the bucket bits\n  // The remaining bits start from position (BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - n_bits_) down to 0\n  // Position is counted from right to left (1-indexed)\n\n  // Starting from the most significant bit (after bucket bits) going down\n  for (int i = BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - n_bits_; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (bset.test(i)) {\n      // Position from the leftmost bit of the remaining portion (1-indexed)\n      return static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_ - i);\n    }\n  }\n  // If all bits are 0, return the maximum position + 1\n  return static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_ + 1);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Adds a value into the HyperLogLog.\n *\n * @param[in] val - value that's added into hyperloglog\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_bits_ < 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> binary = ComputeBinary(hash);\n\n  // Extract the bucket index from the n_bits_ most significant bits\n  uint64_t bucket_index = 0;\n  if (n_bits_ > 0) {\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_bits_; i++) {\n      if (binary.test(BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - i)) {\n        bucket_index |= (1ULL << (n_bits_ - 1 - i));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t pos = PositionOfLeftmostOne(binary);\n\n  // Update the register with the maximum value\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n  if (pos > registers_[bucket_index]) {\n    registers_[bucket_index] = pos;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes cardinality.\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_bits_ < 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  size_t m = registers_.size();  // Number of registers (2^n_bits)\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(registers_[i]));\n  }\n\n  // Apply the HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum\n  double alpha = CONSTANT;\n  double estimate = alpha * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<size_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<std::string>;\n\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":421,"cache_read_input_tokens":42345,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":421,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"aa385926-dc6e-484e-8e9d-79fc154a40b0","uuid":"41b4a6f2-ea28-432d-885a-24c0343c10ca"}

Looking at the tests:\n\n- When n_leading_bits is 1, we have 2 buckets\n- The bucket index comes from the first `n_leading_bits` of the 64-bit hash\n- The \"value\" stored is the position of the leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n- The dense bucket stores the lower 4 bits of the value\n- The overflow bucket stores the upper 3 bits of the value"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":253,"cache_read_input_tokens":48680,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":253,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"aa385926-dc6e-484e-8e9d-79fc154a40b0","uuid":"910dc472-ccb1-46ff-9135-377c692924fe"}

Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits) : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ <= 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    dense_bucket_.resize(1, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  } else {\n    dense_bucket_.resize(1ULL << n_leading_bits_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // Extract bucket index from the n_leading_bits_ most significant bits\n  uint64_t bucket_index = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_index = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits (after the n_leading_bits)\n  // The remaining bits start from bit (63 - n_leading_bits_) down to bit 0\n  uint64_t pos = 0;\n\n  // Mask out the leading bits and find position of leftmost 1\n  int remaining_bits = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n\n  // Check each bit from the most significant of the remaining portion\n  for (int i = remaining_bits - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if ((hash >> i) & 1ULL) {\n      // Position is 1-indexed from the leftmost bit of the remaining portion\n      pos = static_cast<uint64_t>(remaining_bits - i);\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // If no 1 bit found, pos remains 0, we should set it to remaining_bits + 1\n  if (pos == 0) {\n    pos = static_cast<uint64_t>(remaining_bits + 1);\n  }\n\n  // Split the value into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper 3 bits)\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_index].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)].to_ullong();\n  }\n\n  // Reconstruct current value from dense and overflow\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Update if new value is larger\n  if (pos > current_value) {\n    // Split pos into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper 3 bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = pos & 0xF;  // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (pos >> 4) & 0x7;  // Upper 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_index] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    if (new_overflow > 0) {\n      overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n    } else {\n      overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(0);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  size_t m = dense_bucket_.size();  // Number of registers (2^n_leading_bits)\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t value = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(value));\n  }\n\n  // Apply the HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum\n  double alpha = CONSTANT;\n  double estimate = alpha * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();","+    }","+    uint64_t value = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;","+    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(value));","+  }","+","+  // Apply the HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum","+  double alpha = CONSTANT;","+  double estimate = alpha * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;","+","+  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);"," }"," "," template class Group\n//\n//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//\n\n#include \"primer/hyperloglog_presto.h\"\n#include <cmath>\n\nnamespace bustub {\n\n/** @brief Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits) : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ <= 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    dense_bucket_.resize(1, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  } else {\n    dense_bucket_.resize(1ULL << n_leading_bits_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // Extract bucket index from the n_leading_bits_ most significant bits\n  uint64_t bucket_index = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_index = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits (after the n_leading_bits)\n  // The remaining bits start from bit (63 - n_leading_bits_) down to bit 0\n  uint64_t pos = 0;\n\n  // Mask out the leading bits and find position of leftmost 1\n  int remaining_bits = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n\n  // Check each bit from the most significant of the remaining portion\n  for (int i = remaining_bits - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if ((hash >> i) & 1ULL) {\n      // Position is 1-indexed from the leftmost bit of the remaining portion\n      pos = static_cast<uint64_t>(remaining_bits - i);\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // If no 1 bit found, pos remains 0, we should set it to remaining_bits + 1\n  if (pos == 0) {\n    pos = static_cast<uint64_t>(remaining_bits + 1);\n  }\n\n  // Split the value into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper 3 bits)\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_index].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)].to_ullong();\n  }\n\n  // Reconstruct current value from dense and overflow\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Update if new value is larger\n  if (pos > current_value) {\n    // Split pos into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper 3 bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = pos & 0xF;  // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (pos >> 4) & 0x7;  // Upper 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_index] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    if (new_overflow > 0) {\n      overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n    } else {\n      overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_index)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(0);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  size_t m = dense_bucket_.size();  // Number of registers (2^n_leading_bits)\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t value = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(value));\n  }\n\n  // Apply the HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum\n  double alpha = CONSTANT;\n  double estimate = alpha * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}
